Two consequences, both by the letter of C60 (2): (a) a camera the WORKER faulted first (`CAMERA_STREAM_ENDED`) stays faulted when its push returns inside the session - the worker never clears a camera condition, so it ends when the next session records (R-4); (b) a fault the WATCH raised first and the worker then also says is cleared by the watch when it sees the source back, while the forwarder's own table still holds it (its later clear is a no-op at the engine). row V4 (agent V4) | row 191c - the seal (`apps/core-server/src/sealing/pipeline/pipeline.ts:745`, `sealing-host/session-reader.ts`; NOT edited by me) | THE SIGNED MANIFEST MEMBER FOR AUDIO ABSENT ACROSS A WORKER RESTART LANDED (decision C73); the WRITER is yours. THE EXACT MEMBER: `audio.absentOnRestart` of `session-manifest.json` - OPTIONAL, an array of at least one record `{ earlierAttempt: <whole number >= 1>, laterAttempt: <whole number > earlierAttempt>, absentAtLeastMs: <0..86400000 | null>, absentAtMostMs: <0..86400000 | null> }` (strict: no other key; `ManifestAudioRestartGapSchema`, type `ManifestAudioRestartGap`, bound `MANIFEST_AUDIO_ABSENT_MAX_MS`, all of `@dems/shared/evidence-package`; fields 129-132 of `MANIFEST_V2_OPTIONAL_FIELDS`). ONE RECORD PER RESTART, in the order they happened (a record does not begin before the previous one's later attempt); `absentAtLeastMs <= absentAtMostMs` when both are measured; null = that bound was NOT measured (the audit row `CAPTURE_AUDIO_ABSENT_ON_RESTART` leaves its key out in that case - same values, same bound, held equal by `core/__tests__/writers.test.ts`). HOW TO WRITE IT: hand it in the builder's input - `audio: { trackId, codec, containsIn, sourceIntervals, ...(gaps.length > 0 ? { absentOnRestart: gaps } : {}) }` to `buildUnsignedManifest`. NO RESTART = NO KEY: an empty list is REFUSED (`MANIFEST_INVALID`, member `audio.absentOnRestart`), and `undefined` is dropped by the builder, so a session without a restart seals byte for byte the manifest it sealed before. The member is inside `manifestSha256` and under the signature (changing one number breaks V-03). A VERIFIER THAT PREDATES V4 REFUSES A MANIFEST THAT CARRIES THE MEMBER (strict schema, V-01): only packages of a session that had a restart carry it - name that in README known limit 7 when you rewrite it. NOT in the manifest: the device, the instant of an interval, and the `timeline` of a restarted session (row 191b's first remainder: `recordingDurationMs` still spans the session - the member is how the difference is accounted for, the timeline itself is unchanged) | blocking: no for the build; yes for the gate of the first full session (M6)

row N2 (agent N2; record section 6) | THE CONTROLLER, then rows N11 and N12 - A RULING IS NEEDED | RULE 5 OF THE MEMBER AGAINST A WORKER THAT IS KILLED. Built as the design words it (8.4, rule 5): every listed run ends at or before the largest `timelineSamples` of its attempt, and the listed runs plus `runsNotListedSamples` are at most the attempt's `fedInsertedSamples`. A run row is written when the worker's record ARRIVES; the account of a killed worker is its LAST PERIODIC one (up to 60 s older). So a run that ended after that account lies PAST the account's `timelineSamples`: the manifest's schema refuses it and the seal stops (`MANIFEST_INVALID`) on an honest session - fail-closed, but a session that cannot be sealed. Ways out: (a) the seal's reader lists for a `NOT_CLOSED` account only the runs that end at or before its `timelineSamples` and counts the later ones nowhere (loses signed positions); (b) rule 5's two bounds hold for `CLOSED` accounts only (a relaxing: every package valid today stays valid); (c) the capture session states the Core's last telemetry `timelineSamples` (the design allows "a second, lower-trust figure", SM-29 (4)). Kept strict because a later relaxing breaks no sealed package and a later tightening would. | blocking: no for N2; YES before row N12 closes
